```text
Use case: stylized-concept
Asset type: GameW Chapter 3 final task/dungeon image, final project asset
Primary request: Generate a clean 3840x2160 4K landscape image for "地下照影母局三钥裁决" at the map location "地下照影母局" in "照影城".
Scene/backdrop: The final underground control chamber beneath Zhaoying City, a vast low-magic registry-machine built from bronze bells, wooden record racks, stone channels, hanging tie-plates, pulley bridges, and circular archive galleries. At the center stands a large mother-system dais with three distinct key stations: a medical needle-key station with herb trays and treatment vessels, a bronze array-key station with exposed gears and bell cords, and a heart-key station represented by witness tokens, companion keepsakes, and public evidence bundles. Multiple small groups stand around the chamber deciding the system's fate: officials, jianghu allies, ghost-market figures, healers, artisans, border riders, and a lone dark old-order silhouette near a side door. The image should show the final choice between destroying, reforming, stealing, preserving treatment, public archive, or taking control; no readable text.
Style/medium: high quality AAA wuxia CRPG finale concept art, painterly realism, grounded low-magic machinery, matching Chapter 3 key visual: imperial stone, dark tiled architecture underground, bronze mechanisms, lantern red accents, cold blue shaft light, oppressive institutional scale.
Composition/framing: 16:9 cinematic landscape, playable final arena layout with foreground broken bridge and evidence bundles, midground three-key central dais, background towering archive rings and bell arrays disappearing into darkness; tiny scale figures only, no close-up portraits.
Lighting/mood: solemn, dangerous, final moral裁决 atmosphere, copper glow and deep shadows, no gore focus.
Strict constraints: no readable text, no letters, no labels, no UI, no watermark, no logo, no signature, no modern objects, no guns, no electricity, no fantasy monsters.
```

```text
Character/backdrop: A low-magic wuxia poison doctor in travel-worn layered robes, carrying a lacquer medicine chest, needle rolls, small ceramic vials, herb packets, brass trigger cords, and a short staff used to guide a squat wooden medicine puppet. The puppet is clearly mechanical/ritual, not a living monster: wood joints, cloth talismans, tiny clay medicine furnace in its torso, visible cords, no modern robotics. Set them in a rain-dark village lane or old clinic courtyard that fits GameW Chapter 1-3 tone, with herb smoke and lantern light. Style/medium: high quality AAA wuxia CRPG character concept art, painterly realism, grounded low-magic, GameW style: weathered cloth, practical belts, herbal medicine textures, muted ink greens and browns, warm lantern highlights, no superhero costume.
Composition/framing: 16:9 landscape character concept sheet, full-body three-quarter stance centered, medicine puppet slightly forward to one side, small callout-like prop arrangement but without any text or labels; clear silhouette and readable equipment.
Lighting/mood: secretive healer-poisoner, morally ambiguous, tactical and eerie but not monstrous, no gore.
Strict constraints: no readable text, no labels, no UI, no watermark, no logo, no signature, no modern objects, no guns, no fantasy monsters, no gore focus.
```
